六、台灣—AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健 
9 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較上月增加 1 分，燈號
續呈紅燈，其中工業及服務業加班工時，受惠 AI 相關產業生產動能維持熱絡，
由綠燈轉呈黃紅燈，其餘八項燈號均維持不變。另領先與同時指標皆呈上升，顯
示國內經濟動能維持穩健。 
主計總處 10 月 30 日公布台灣第三季經濟成長率為 14.36%，主要受 AI 需求
強勁帶動出口及企業投資大幅成長，民間消費亦維持熱絡，顯示外需動能正逐步
向內需擴散，整體經濟呈現內外皆熱。展望未來，AI 需求、半導體相關投資及企
業資本支出持續擴張，加上年底消費旺季，可望支撐短期景氣表現；惟今年經濟
高速成長墊高比較基期，明年增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成
長動能向傳統產業擴散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

科技股漲多修正，資金輪動主導盤勢
2026 年 8 月
7 月全球股市呈現鮮明的區域輪動，資金由先前漲幅集中的亞洲 AI 供應鏈，轉向估值與今年以來
表現相對落後的市場。以美元計價觀察，MSCI 中國、東協及拉丁美洲為當月前三強。相較之下，
MSCI 新興亞洲與新興市場跌幅較大，MSCI 新興亞洲中南韓與台灣合計權重接近六成，7 月南韓半
導體及 AI 硬體股因估值偏高、資本支出回收疑慮與槓桿部位平倉而急跌，成為指數的主要壓力來源。
即便 7 月新興亞洲與新興市場出現回檔，但年初迄今表現仍佳，反觀中國與印度仍分別下跌 8.0%及

9.0%。因此，我們認為 7 月盤勢較接近強勢 AI 題材暫時性獲利了結，而先前落後市場低基期補漲的
資金輪動行情，尚不足以判定全球股市原有多頭結構已全面反轉。
七月全球債市整體偏弱，僅新興當地債及亞洲高收益債維持正報酬。以美元計價觀察，新興美元
債下跌 1.30% 為最弱券種。當月債市壓力主要來自能源價格波動、通膨黏著性及聯準會政策不確定
性。Fed 於 7/29 決議將利率維持在 3.50%至 3.75%，但三位地區聯準銀行總裁主張立即升息一碼，
官方聲明亦明確指出通膨仍高於 2%目標，能源供給衝擊持續推升部分價格。當日會後 30 年期美債
殖利率創 2007 年以來高位，但 2 年期殖利率卻下降，反映市場並非單純押注短期升息，而是提高對
長期通膨、政策可信度與期限溢酬的要求，殖利率曲線因而明顯陡峭化。
我們認為，七月股市修正主要來自折現率、評價與籌碼調整，目前尚未出現 AI 需求下降的證據。
半導體類股近期修正後，評價壓力已部分釋放，但技術面仍未形成明確底部。既有核心持股不宜因短
線波動而全面減碼，但建議降低高評價、高擁擠度及高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集中度。此外，投資人

可以定時定額或在拉回時分批布局具獲利潛力的台、日股市。韓國股市則受三星電子、SK 海力士雙
巨頭合計占指數權重逾半，個股風險易被結構性放大，建議投資人於短線反彈適度調節相關曝險。
在聯準會減少前瞻指引、利率路徑更依賴數據環境下，市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將提
高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較大
波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給
增加等負面因素。因此，建議債券布局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸
引力的息收，亦能降低短端政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

美國股市：AI 成長主線未變，短線估值風險升溫
七月美股受伊朗戰爭不確定性、聯準會態度轉鷹干擾，股市出現巨幅震盪。雖財報盈餘表現強勁，
但市場開始觀察資本支出擴張壓迫科技龍頭企業的現金流。指數上揚主要由微軟、蘋果、亞馬遜、輝
達、埃克森美孚、博通、摩根大通銀行、雪佛龍、萬事達卡、威士卡等企業帶動，表現相對弱勢的族
群包括美光、特斯拉、英特爾、應用材料、科林研發、科磊、晟碟、超微半導體、卡特彼勒、康寧等。
美國經濟整體仍維持穩健，但第二季 GDP 年化季增率由 2.1%放緩至 1.5%，主要反映進口增加、
企業庫存調整及政府支出下降。民間消費仍是第二季經濟成長的主要支撐，年化季增率由第一季的

0.5%大幅回升至 3.2%，其中耐久財消費成長 6.8%、非耐久財成長 4.4%，服務消費亦由 0.5%回升
至 2.2%。此外，第二季民間投資成長 3.0%，其中非住宅投資成長 8.4%，顯示 AI 資料中心、軟體與
研發支出仍持續擴張。另一方面，第二季進口大幅成長 11.5%，增幅主要集中於電信設備、半導體及
工業設備等資本財，反映企業資本支出需求仍相當強勁。
能源價格回落有助通膨降溫，但整體物價壓力仍不容忽視。6 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 月增率同步放
緩，其中整體 PCE 轉為月減 0.1%，反映能源價格明顯回落，足以抵銷其他商品與服務價格上漲壓力，
年增率為 3.7%，仍高於 Fed 目標。核心 PCE 月增率亦降至 0.1%，創近數月低點；其中，聯準會高
度關注的超級核心服務通膨出現降溫，主要受投資組合管理費漲幅由 4.5%大幅降至 0.6%，以及旅館
住宿與機票價格回落帶動。不過，醫療保健價格仍維持穩健增長，且部分商品價格持續受到關稅影響
而上揚，加上美伊戰事仍未落幕，通膨風險依舊存在。

本輪由 AI 投資帶動的「AI 通膨」，對整體 CPI 的直接推升有限，卻有助於川普推動「美國製造」
政策目標。2024 年以來，全球雲端服務業者大幅擴張 AI 資本支出，帶動高階晶片、記憶體需求急升，
不過，電視、電腦周邊及手機合計占美國 CPI 權重偏低，即使相關產品價格上漲，對整體通膨的直接
影響仍相對有限。相較之下，AI 通膨對產業結構與供應鏈在地化的影響更值得關注。台積電毛利率
上升同時也擴大在美國資本支出、美光也在記憶體價格回升後重新啟動紐約廠建設、輝達則進一步投
資光通訊與先進封裝業者，協助相關產能在美國落地。唯有當價格與需求長期維持在足以支撐獲利的
水準，企業才有能力承擔高額資本支出，並推動製造業供應鏈回美擴張。
目前標普 500 指數續創新高，本益比已升至 25.8 倍、股價淨值比達 5.8 倍，短期評價偏高。雖
然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但 AI 技術與競爭格局變化快速，領先廠商尚未與後續追
趕者明顯拉開差距。短期仍需留意市場過熱與估值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：經濟溫和復甦，評價仍具吸引力
七月歐股受伊朗戰爭不確定性干擾而震盪。泛歐 600 指數中，能源與金融股表現相對強勢，包括
殼牌、匯豐、道達爾能源、BP、畢爾巴鄂比斯開銀行、聯合聖保羅銀行及法國巴黎銀行等；相較之
下，半導體、科技硬體與部分防禦型消費股表現偏弱，包括艾司摩爾、英飛凌、意法半導體、諾基亞、
雀巢等。整體市場焦點由科技股回歸能源與金融族群。
歐元區第 2 季 GDP 初值季增率回升至 0.4%，顯示經濟動能在第 1 季停滯後逐步修復。不過，成
員國表現仍明顯分化：西班牙季增 0.7%，持續領先主要經濟體，主要受惠於服務業復甦與觀光需求

回升；德國 GDP 季增率則放緩至 0.2%，反映製造業疲弱與出口需求不足仍限制復甦力道；法國則
由前季萎縮轉為成長 0.2%，顯示內需逐步改善。整體而言，第 2 季 GDP 回升主要受消費改善、投
資回穩及政府支出支撐，但復甦力道仍屬溫和。
7 月歐元區通膨率受能源價格反彈影響，小幅回升至 2.9%，已連續 5 個月高於 ECB 的 2.0%目標，
但仍低於 5 月 3.2%的高點。核心通膨則升至 2.5%，主要受服務業與非能源工業品價格同步走揚帶
動，其中服務業通膨由 3.2%升至 3.3%，非能源工業品由 0.7%升至 0.9%；食品與菸酒年增率則續降
至 1.2%，反映食品價格降溫趨勢延續。整體而言，物價壓力僅溫和回升，尚未出現全面加速跡象。
ECB 在七月利率決策會議維持政策利率 2.25%不變，重申將採取逐次會議、數據依賴的決策模
式，不預先承諾未來利率路徑，官方仍維持經濟成長風險偏下行的評估。雖然歐元區整體通膨較前期
高點放緩，但中東衝突再度升溫，推升原油與天然氣價格，加上目前全球能源與天然氣庫存水位偏低，

未來能源價格變化及其對通膨的傳導效果，仍將是市場關注重點。ECB 刻意未恢復前瞻指引，並持續
強調後續數據的重要性，反映其希望在高度不確定環境下保留政策彈性，因此不排除 9 月升息的可能。
7 月歐元區綜合 PMI 回升至 51.9，重返榮枯線之上並創 5 個月新高，整體景氣動能有所改善。
其中，服務業 PMI 升至 51.6，受惠於需求回升而重返擴張區間；製造業 PMI 亦由 51.4 升至 51.9，
加上新訂單 5 個月來首度恢復成長，進一步支撐景氣修復。
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦，泛歐 600 指數成分股多為具備全球布局優勢的產業龍頭。目前指數
本益比約 17.5 倍、股價淨值比約 2.5 倍，評價仍屬合理。雖然能源價格高檔與通膨再升溫風險仍需
留意，但在評價合理、伊朗戰爭有望於第三季落幕的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

日本股市：日圓匯率短線急升，股市評價合理
七月日股受地緣政治風險與全球科技股回檔影響，日經 225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，金融、外銷
與大型權值股表現相對強勢，包括三菱 UFJ 金融集團、日立製作所、索尼、豐田、三井住友金融、
東京海上控股、瑞穗金融、三菱商事等；半導體與電子零組件族群則相對承壓，包括東京威力科創、
村田製作所、鎧俠、瑞薩、Disco、信越化學等。整體市場焦點由科技股轉向外銷股與升息預期受惠
的金融股。
7 月東京 CPI 年增率自前月的 1.7%攀升至 2.0%、東京核心 CPI（扣除生鮮食品）年增率為 1.9%、

扣除食品與能源後的 CPI 年增率則為 1.3%。商品價格年增率由前月的 2.3%升至 2.9%，是通膨回升
的主要原因；服務價格年增率則小幅升至 1.2%，顯示薪資成長仍推升服務價格。分項來看，本月通
膨上行主要來自能源與公用事業費用，反映電費與瓦斯費價格上漲；同時，食品價格年增率也由前月
的 3.8%回升至 4.0%。展望後市，日本政府 7~9 月重啟電費補貼，將抑制能源價格。不過，近期國
際油價再度攀高，可能抵消部分政策效果。
日本央行七月利率決策會議維持政策利率不變，委員以 8 比 1 同意將政策利率維持於 1.0%。僅
高田創委員主張將政策利率調高至 1.25%。就景氣現況來看，日本經濟仍呈現溫和復甦態勢，儘管部
分經濟活動受到中東局勢影響而略顯疲弱，但出口與工業生產大致保持平穩。受惠於全球 AI 相關需
求強勁成長，企業固定投資仍溫和增加；同時，私人消費在就業與所得環境改善支撐下仍具韌性。日
銀也將 2026 財年 GDP 年增率預估由前次的 0.5%小幅上調至 0.6%，2027 財年由 0.7%上調至 0.8%。

近期美日聯手干預日圓匯率，日本財務大臣片山皋月表示，已協同美國財政部實施買進日圓的
干預措施，以因應近來日圓匯率過度波動及失序走勢，且不排除進一步採取協同干預。美國財長貝森
特也表示，將毫不猶豫參與進一步的聯合日圓干預行動，並強烈支持日本採取果斷措施，修正日圓嚴
重低估的問題。美國總統川普則稱，日本需要一些幫助。此次為日本與美國 15 年來首次就日圓採取
聯合行動，兩國共同干預匯市確實對日圓形成支撐，突襲式干預也有助引發投資人空單回補，暫時延
續日圓升值趨勢。不過，目前日美政策利率差距仍高達 2.5%，若日本央行無法加快升息步伐，中長
期匯價重新回歸利差基本面，日圓仍可能重拾偏弱格局。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政策利多支撐，
儘管指數已明顯上漲，目前本益比約 19.5 倍、股價淨值比約 1.8 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震
盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：AI 出口與接單動能續強，8 月聚焦財報兌現與供應鏈擴散
7 月台股因前期漲幅較大、科技股評價偏高，加上市場重新檢視 AI 投資回收期、企業獲利能否符
合高預期，外資調節、融資去化與槓桿資金停損進一步放大波動，資金也由高評價電子股轉向較具防
禦性的族群。不過，這波修正有助消化過高評價與浮動籌碼，降低市場過熱風險；後續隨雲端業者財
報持續驗證 AI 資本支出，半導體、伺服器及先進封裝需求延續，市場焦點可望逐步回歸企業獲利與
產業基本面。6 月景氣對策信號升至 41 分、連續第 7 個月亮紅燈，製造業銷售與加班工時同步轉強，

顯示 AI 動能已由出口延伸至生產、設備投資與勞動投入。雖然產業間景氣感受仍有落差，但下半年
電子產品備貨需求、半導體資本支出及 AI 基礎建設持續推進，仍可望支撐出口與企業獲利，為後續
盤勢提供正向動能。
當前臺灣經濟正處於生產擴張的黃金期。最新公布的總體經濟數據與半導體龍頭營收呈現全面共
振，印證全球 AI 資本支出（CapEx）並非流於故事題材，而是實質轉化為台廠的訂單與獲利。儘管
全球金融市場短期受到地緣政治干擾與美債殖利率波動影響，引發台股在高檔震盪，但主計總處大幅
上修 2026 年 GDP 預測至 9.64%。國發會公布 6 月景氣對策信號綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較 5 月
增加 2 分，連續第 7 個月呈現代表景氣熱絡的紅燈，為史上第二長的連續紅燈紀錄，顯示台灣景氣
並非單月跳升，而是已形成一段相對持續的高檔擴張週期。
展望 8 月，台灣景氣仍有望維持高檔，但景氣分數的邊際變化將比燈號本身更值得關注。全球
AI 基礎建設持續擴張，加上下半年進入消費性電子備貨旺季，預期有利半導體、AI 伺服器、先進封

裝、散熱、電源及高速傳輸供應鏈；國內主要半導體業者提高資本支出，也將持續帶動設備、材料及
廠務工程需求。另一方面，景氣分數已接近紅燈區間上緣 45 分，未來即使出口與生產維持強勁，受
到比較基期墊高影響，分數未必持續上升，因此不宜將分數持平或小幅回落直接解讀為景氣反轉。
投資策略上可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但選股宜從題材轉向獲利品質。除先進製程外，8
月可提高對先進封裝、測試介面、AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、及記憶體的關注。IDC 預估 2026 年全球半
導體市場規模將達 1.29 兆美元、年增 52.8%，資料中心半導體營收達 4,771 億美元，意味著 AI 投資
正由 GPU 延伸至記憶體、網路與系統級零組件。不過，若股價已提前反映高成長，8 月仍應留意財
測不及預期、匯率、成本上升及資金集中度過高所引發的波動。

中國股市：復甦力道偏緩，科技自主為結構性題材但仍待驗證
7 月中國經濟延續「總量放緩、科技製造相對抗跌」的分化格局。2026 年上半年 GDP 年增 4.7%，
第二季成長率由第一季的 5.0%放緩至 4.3%；6 月規模以上工業增加值年增 5.3%，其中製造業成長
6.0%，電腦、通信及其他電子設備製造業成長 15.7%。數據顯示 AI 與電子設備提供局部支撐，但內
需疲弱與房地產調整仍未見明顯改善，整體復甦動能偏緩，成長結構對少數產業的依賴度偏高。
科技產業方面，7 月市場焦點轉向半導體設備、AI 模型與記憶體的自主化，惟題材面成分仍高於

基本面。據媒體報導，中國已開始製造國產浸沒式 DUV 微影設備，首批系統預計供應中芯國際、華
虹半導體及長鑫科技，但 2026 年產量可能僅約 5 台，且效能、可靠度與量產驗證仍明顯落後 ASML，
短期象徵意義大於實際產能貢獻。後續能否降低成熟製程與部分多重曝光製程對進口設備的依賴，仍
取決於良率與客戶端驗證結果，對國產蝕刻、薄膜沉積、檢測及零組件的實質拉動亦需時間觀察。
AI 軟體端，月之暗面於 7 月推出 Kimi K3，模型具 2.8 兆參數、100 萬 Token 上下文及原生視覺
理解能力，並採開放權重策略，反映中國大模型競爭已由低價推論延伸至模型能力與開發者生態。惟
開放權重的商業模式尚未證明可持續獲利，應用落地速度亦存在不確定性。若相關應用推展順利，或
有利國產雲端服務、資料中心、伺服器與推論晶片需求；但模型效率提升同時可能壓抑算力採購規模，
市場對 AI 投資的檢視標準將轉向使用效率與變現能力。
記憶體方面，長鑫科技正由一般 DRAM 逐步向 DDR5、LPDDR5X 及 HBM3 延伸，市場研究「估

計」其 2026 年底 DRAM 月產能可能接近 35 萬片晶圓，惟該預估未經證實，實際良率與產品組合仍
待確認。國產 DUV 若逐步導入其產線，或可提高設備取得的自主性，但導入時程與穩定度均具變數。
整體而言，8 月中國市場可留意半導體設備、國產記憶體、AI 模型、資料中心與工業自動化，惟評價
已部分反映預期，投資判斷宜以技術驗證、訂單落地與實際營收貢獻為依據，避免過度追逐自主替代
題材。
展望 8 月，政策環境預計仍以穩增長及培育新動能為主，但政策效果傳導有限，市場表現料將持
續分化。中國上半年新動能，包括高端製造、數位經濟與現代服務業，對經濟成長的貢獻超過四成；
第三季製造業雖有 AI 相關產品、綠色科技出口及政策支持，惟房地產、基礎建設投資及中小企業需
求疲弱仍是主要壓抑因素。

拉美市場：巴西通膨改善支撐市場表現，惟總統大選不確定性仍存
7 月全球股市普遍面臨回檔，然而拉美股市表現卻相對抗跌，主因巴西市場先前已因降息預期收
斂而出現修正，而近期巴西通膨數據及通膨預期出現改善，使市場對於巴西通膨問題的擔憂有所緩
解，也帶動巴西指數表現相對硬挺，並為整體拉美市場指數提供支撐。
7 月巴西通膨數據出現改善，主要來自食品與燃料價格回落。7 月巴西 IPCA-15 消費者物價指
數月增 0.06%，明顯低於市場預期的 0.22%，年增率亦由 6 月的 4.80% 降至 4.52%。其中，家庭
食品價格由 6 月月增 0.87% 轉為月減 1.14%；燃料價格整體月減 0.90%，汽油價格月減 0.68%，

帶動整體通膨放緩，並降低市場對近期巴西物價再度急升的疑慮。
此外，本次巴西通膨數據改善亦反映在核心通膨與服務通膨上，使優於預期的數據更接近結構性
通膨改善。五種核心通膨指標平均月增 0.21%，年增率由 4.45% 降至 4.35%。同時，服務通膨月
增 0.41%，低於市場預期的 0.59%，年增率也由 6.25% 降至 5.95%；然而，勞力密集型服務年增
率仍由 7.06% 升至 7.30%，顯示通膨壓力尚未全面消退，後續仍需持續觀察。
另一方面，儘管巴西通膨數據改善，仍未逆轉市場對巴西央行降息幅度受限的預期。截至 7 月 27
日的巴西央行每週市場調查顯示，2026 年市場通膨預期較前期下降 3 個基點至 5.12%，但 2027
年仍上升 2 個基點至 4.22%，2028 年亦連續第二週上升 2 個基點至 3.80%，2029 年則維持
3.50%；四個年度皆持續高於巴西央行 3.0%的通膨目標。此外，市場對 2026、2027、2028 及 2029
年底巴西基準利率的預期，亦分別維持在 14.00%、12.00%、10.50%及 10.00%，顯示市場對通膨

的疑慮仍限制巴西利率正常化的速度與幅度。
除了通膨之外，巴西總統大選是影響拉美市場的另一項關鍵因素。巴西民調機構 Nexus 於 7 月
全國民調顯示，在總統大選第一輪中，魯拉以 42% 領先波索納洛的 33%，與 7 月中旬前次調查
的 42% 對 34% 大致相同；第二輪則為 47% 對 43%，魯拉仍領先 4 個百分點。兩輪調查均由
魯拉領先，顯示其整體勝率仍較高。此外，包含 Datafolha、Genial／Quaest 等民調皆顯示目前魯
拉贏面較高。由於魯拉並非市場偏好的候選人，其較高的連任機率可能壓抑巴西股市表現。因此，儘
管近期巴西通膨降溫有助支撐整體拉美市場行情，巴西選舉風險仍可能抵銷部分利多；同時，在美伊
衝突仍具不確定性的情況下，巴西降息行情亦料將受到壓抑，後市表現預期將落後於全球市場。

科技類股：CSP 廠商資本支出持續加碼，市場更加關注獲利轉化能力
全球科技產業的主要成長動能仍集中於 AI 基礎建設，四大雲端服務供應商資本支出均維持高檔
或進一步上修，顯示資料中心、AI 伺服器、網路設備及雲端容量的擴建週期尚未降溫。Microsoft、
Amazon 與 Google 的雲端業務均維持強勁成長，且訂單、產能需求與客戶使用量持續增加，反映企
業導入生成式 AI、主權 AI 及 AI 工廠的趨勢仍在深化。市場焦點也逐漸由單一 AI 晶片出貨，延伸至
高速網路、記憶體、CPU、電力與散熱等整體資料中心架構。只要 CSP 廠商未明顯下修資本支出，

AI 硬體供應鏈的訂單能見度便可望維持，NVIDIA、AMD 及相關半導體與設備廠商仍是本輪科技投
資循環的主要受惠者。
不過，市場對科技公司的評價標準正由投入多少資本支出轉向能否將投資轉化為營收、獲利與現
金流。Microsoft 在大幅增加資本支出的同時，營運現金流與自由現金流仍維持成長，Google 雲端
業務的獲利也明顯提升，說明部分 AI 投資已逐步進入商業化階段；Amazon 則受惠 AWS 成長加速，
雲端業務持續扮演主要獲利來源。相較之下，Meta 與特斯拉因 AI 研發、基礎建設及新事業投入快
速增加，面臨費用上升、利潤率下降與自由現金流承壓的問題。未來科技股表現可能進一步分化，市
場將更重視 AI 收入成長、產能利用率、投資回收期及折舊費用，而非單純將高額資本支出視為利多。
從供應鏈角度來看，AI 需求正由 GPU 逐步擴散至 CPU、記憶體、晶圓代工、類比晶片與工業半
導體。Intel 資料中心與 AI 業務明顯加速，顯示 CPU 在 AI 伺服器的資料處理、系統控制與通用運算

架構中仍具重要地位。記憶體價格上升雖有利於相關製造商，但也推高 CSP 建置成本，促使雲端業
者重新檢視採購效率與系統配置。整體而言，科技產業有機會由少數 AI 核心企業領漲，逐步轉為伺
服器、網通、儲存、電源、散熱及工業半導體共同受惠的擴散型成長。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但高成長也伴隨高估值與高資本密集度風
險。NVIDIA 的 Blackwell 出貨、Rubin 平台時程、高階記憶體供應與中國市場需求，將是觀察 AI
硬體循環的重要指標；Apple、Meta 與特斯拉則需證明 AI 功能、新裝置及自動化應用能夠轉化為實
際收入。目前 CSP 訂單與雲端需求仍未出現明顯降溫，科技產業基本面維持正向，但隨著折舊、能
源、記憶體及研發成本陸續上升，企業獲利差異可能擴大。未來較具優勢的企業，將不只是擁有先進
技術或積極投入 AI，而是能建立穩定商業模式，並將 AI 投資有效轉化為營收、利潤與現金流的公司。

能源類股：美伊衝突帶動油價波動，實體供應仍未見改善
7 月油價波動劇烈，主因美伊衝突與停火談判反覆。美伊在 7 月中旬恢復互相攻擊、美國重啟
對伊朗港口的海上封鎖，且荷莫茲海峽再度發生商船遇襲事件，帶動布蘭特原油一度重返每桶 100
美元以上；然而其後美方暫停攻擊並重啟外交談判後，油價又回落至約 80 美元，這顯示當前油價
變動主要反映市場對於衝突升降溫的預期，而非實際供應已恢復正常。
彭博分析指出，目前石油實物市場尚未出現明顯改善，荷莫茲海峽可觀察到的油輪數量仍遠低於
戰前，許多船隻通行時仍持續關閉船舶自動識別系統，且保險公司、租船人及船東也維持審慎態度，

顯示商業航運、海運保險、運費及出口流量均未恢復正常。航運資料公司 Windward 7 月 30 日的
船舶追蹤也顯示，荷莫茲海峽當日僅有 7 艘船舶進入波斯灣、4 艘離開，整體運量相較於戰前仍有
極大的落差，故一旦美伊談判受阻，地緣政治風險溢價仍有迅速回升的可能性。
另一方面，胡塞武裝宣布將封鎖沙烏地阿拉伯有關的船舶運輸，並對紅海曼德海峽通行發出威
脅，也加劇油價上行風險。彭博指出，沙烏地阿拉伯雖可利用東西輸油管，將原先在荷莫茲海峽受阻
的每日約 500 萬桶原油運往紅海沿岸的延布港，但經由紅海運往亞洲的船舶仍須通過曼德海峽，因
此原油出口風險只是由波斯灣轉移至紅海。船舶追蹤公司 Windward 截至 8 月 3 日的統計顯示，
7 月 20 日後曼德海峽船隻流量下降 22%，油輪更減少 40%，顯示經紅海的替代路線仍面臨威脅。
然而，高油價帶來的需求破壞也會限制油價上漲空間。OPEC 7 月月報將 2026 年全球石油需求
量下修至每日 1.0594 億桶，需求年增量由每日 97 萬桶降至 78 萬桶；2027 年需求年增量雖上

修至每日 194 萬桶，但上修主因 2026 年基期較低的緣故，並非終端需求出現改善。
在此背景下，彭博研究將油價分為三種情境：若外交協議落地、荷莫茲航運與保險恢復正常，布
蘭特原油可能回到每桶 65 至 80 美元；若戰事僅暫停、實物供應持續受阻，則較符合每桶 90 至
110 美元的衝突僵局情境；若荷莫茲與曼德海峽同時受阻，油價可能升至每桶 140 美元以上。
展望 8 月，川普宣布將和伊朗展開新一輪談判，儘管隨即遭到伊朗否認，且荷莫茲海峽仍處於受阻
狀態，然而我們認為隨著期中選舉時程逼近，川普將可能暫緩對伊朗的行動，藉此壓低油價挽救選情，
在此情境下能源類股將會轉趨區間震盪整理，目前中性看待後市表現。

黃金類股：央行配置需求仍強，惟 ETF 資金流出壓抑短線表現
7 月金價保持在區間內震盪整理，儘管美元走強及美債殖利率攀升持續為金價帶來壓力，然而來
自全球央行的買盤也如我們先前所料的為金價帶來了有力的支撐。
世界黃金協會報告指出，2026 年第二季，全球黃金總需求為 1,269 噸，與 2025 年同期持平；
累計 2026 年上半年，全球黃金總需求年增 2%至 2,522 噸，需求金額創下 3,800 億美元新高。與此
同時，2026 年第二季倫敦金銀市場協會黃金午後定盤價平均為每盎司 4,506.29 美元，雖較第一季下

跌 8%，但仍較 2025 年同期上漲 37%。這顯示第二季金價雖自高檔回落，但全球黃金需求並未同步
轉弱，尤其央行買盤回升更成為支撐市場的主要力量。
第二季全球央行合計購買 289 噸黃金，歷經第一季的放緩後，購金規模再次重新回到過去四年
的高檔水準。高盛分析則預估，全球央行 5 月購金量達到 81 噸，其中中國購買 48 噸，是當月最大
的可識別買家；經季節調整後，全球央行近三個月月均購金量達到 67 噸，約為 2022 年前每月 17
噸平均值的四倍。上述數據顯示，即使在金價走勢低迷的狀況下，全球央行對於黃金的承接力道仍強。
進一步觀察央行持續購金的原因，官方貨幣與金融機構論壇（OMFIF）2026 年針對 74 家央行
及 16 家公共退休與主權基金進行的調查顯示，儲備多元化仍是央行持有黃金最主要的原因。此外，
有 51%的受訪者將防範地緣政治風險列為考量，比例較 2024 年增加 11 個百分點；同時，79%的受
訪央行認為全球貨幣體系正朝向多極化發展。調查結果顯示，各國央行正逐步降低對單一儲備貨幣的

依賴，並增持黃金以因應制裁、資產凍結及貨幣體系分裂風險，由此也可見，本波全球央行買盤更接
近長期儲備調整，而非短線對於金價的追逐。
然而對於利率較為敏感的私人投資資金，降低黃金配置趨勢仍持續為金價帶來明顯壓力。世界黃
金協會數據顯示，2026 年第二季全球黃金 ETF 淨流出 45 噸，6 月單月再流出 89 億美元；其中，北
美黃金 ETF 上半年累計流出 77 億美元。造成北美資金流出的主因，在於聯準會態度偏向鷹派，加上
美伊衝突推升通膨疑慮，帶動實質利率與美元走高，進而提高持有黃金的機會成本。
展望後市，儘管短線上金價表現持續承壓，然而中長線看，當前市場已定價到明年底前 Fed 將
升息 2 碼的鷹派走勢，若後續美伊衝突有所緩和，使市場升息預期降溫，黃金 ETF 資金賣壓有望出
現改善，屆時搭配央行長期買盤，黃金仍有望重啟新一波漲勢，故 我們對黃金類股後市維持中性看法。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：Fed 鷹派暫停推升長率，投資仍以 3~10 年期美債/投級債為主
FOMC 7/29 以 9 比 3 票將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.50%～3.75%。Hammack、Kashkari
及 Logan 三名委員反對維持利率不變，主張本次即升息 1 碼。會後聲明指出美國經濟活動仍以穩健
速度擴張，資本投資及生產力成長強勁，但通膨仍高於 2%目標，部分反映能源等供給衝擊。
Fed 主席 Warsh 強調，本次按兵不動並非聯準會行動遲緩或政策慣性，並重申不存在高於 2%
的「軟性通膨目標」。超過五年的高通膨不可能因單月物價小幅下降便獲得解決，聯準會將持續履行

物價穩定責任，必要時不會猶豫採取行動。華許同時指出，距離上次會議的 42 天內，美債殖利率曲
線各期限的名目與實質殖利率均明顯上升，部分升幅已位居近 20 年的前十分位數。他認為，聯準會
減少前瞻指引後，市場開始依據經濟數據自行調整價格。此番談話並非表示聯準會受制於市場，而是
暗示金融市場利率上升已產生部分緊縮效果，使本次不必立即升息。華許指出，AI 相關高科技設備
及軟體投資近四季增長率接近 20%，有助於製造業產出及未來生產力，但記憶體、邏輯晶片及相關
AI 基礎建設價格亦同步上升。FOMC 已開始討論，相關漲價究竟只是特定產業的供給衝擊，或可能
演變為更廣泛的通膨壓力。
債市對此次 FOMC 決議的反應呈現長端領跌、殖利率曲線明顯陡峭化。此一走勢顯示，市場雖
因 Fed 未立即升息而降低短端利率壓力，卻同步提高對能源通膨、財政供給、期限溢酬及政策可信
度的風險定價。利率期貨對 9 月升息的定價在決議後一度升至約 75%，稍後回落至約 6 成，代表市
場並未排除升息，而是等待後續通膨與就業數據確認。8 月沒有例行 FOMC 利率會議，債市定價將

更加依賴經濟數據。由於 Warsh 減少前瞻指引，單一數據對升息機率及短端殖利率的影響可能較過
去放大，預期 8 月殖利率仍將維持高檔震盪。
8 月債市的核心矛盾在於「殖利率已具吸引力，但長端風險尚未完全穩定」。我們認為偏高的殖
利率可提供較佳息收及價格緩衝；若油價維持高檔、核心通膨降溫停滯或美國公債供給增加，20～
30 年期債券仍可能承受期限溢酬上升壓力。貝萊德亦指出，目前美債實質殖利率已具吸引力，但收
益機會仍應透過存續期間控制與券種選擇取得，而非全面延長年期。
在未來聯準會減少前瞻指引、利率路徑更依賴數據環境下，市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將
提高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較
大波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供
給增加等風險。因此，建議債券布局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸
引力的息收，亦能降低短端政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

MPI hosted its 3Q26 earnings call on November 16. Key takeaways:
There could be further upside in 2027/28 capacity expansion: The company
reiterated that it would add at least 2.5mn pins for MEMS and 0.5mn pins for VPC in
2027. It may raise the numbers after its budget plan is finalized in December. MPI
expects to maintain aggressive capacity expansion in 2028, given strong demand.

2027 guidance appears conservative: The company guided for 2027 revenue to be
up at least 50% YoY with gross margin >60% –   conservative vs. consensus
expectations of 2027 revenue doubling. However, we expect MPI to beat its own
guidance  and raise it gradually, per previous practice of amendment.
In qualification for second insertion 2 customer: The company is  still in
qualification for insertion 2. However, it confirmed it has a second significant

customer; the schedule of the qualification could be similar to the first one. MPI said
it would be the only supplier for the first  customer's  insertion 2 process. CPO could
make up a double-digit share of 2028 total revenue.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

n Slower-than-expected AI demand

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

研 究 報 告                
力旺(3529.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
先進與成熟製程同步發酵，營運逐季創高至 2027 年無虞 
 投資評價與建議 
公司地位居產業頂端，全球布局完善，先進製程貢獻推升與成熟製程漲價同步
推營運向上，此外，隨著Caliptra規範推升更多客戶導入公司產品，PUF有望
成為營運主⼒，⻑期營運成⻑無虞，近期股價因系統性風險影響反而浮現投資
價值。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26先進製程與成熟製程同步推升營收與獲利同創新高：受惠先進製程單價
提升更有助營收認列，及成熟製程漲價效應持續發酵，推估營收13.2億元，
QoQ+8.7%，EPS 9.45元。 
營運逐季成⻑將由2026年延續至2027年：展望未來，1)來自先進製程貢獻持
續發酵，由於先進製程單價更高，單⼀授權⾦單價數倍於平均，營收助益將更
大，未來權利⾦可望明顯提升；2)成熟製程回溫：因AI周邊的外溢效果帶動晶
圓代工廠產能利用率提升，自2Q26開始逐步漲價，而晶圓代工將持續漲價至
2027年，⼒旺自3Q26開始同步受惠；3)PUF自2Q25起，授權⾦及權利⾦貢獻
開始放大，其中大部分來自先進製程，隨著 AI 持續蓬勃發展，伴隨全球性資
安事件層出不窮，硬體安全需求有望加速進展，無論是Caliptra開始逐步建立
相關規範或Hardware Root of Trust正從選配變成標配，PUF潛在營運驚人。
整體而言，2026年營運逐季持續成⻑並續創新高將延續至2027年。 
 投資風險 
先進製程進展不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況 
3Q26營收連四季創新高，獲利符合預期：1)受惠先進製程貢獻提升，授權⾦續創新高，其中PUF授權⾦1.6億元，
再創新高，顯示需求持續發酵；2)受惠成熟製程漲價，權利⾦7.7億元也創新高，其中Neofuse權利⾦5.4億元、PUF
權利⾦0.14億元、MTP權利⾦0.25億元，同創新高，整體3Q26營收12.1億元，QoQ+10.4%，明顯超越10億元規
模且連四季創新高，費用4.5億元則控制有成，EPS 8.56元，優於預期。 
4Q26先進製程與成熟製程同步貢獻，營收與獲利有望同創新高：10月營收7.26億元創新高，主要來自權利⾦5.94
億元(創新高)，除了先進製程單價提升更有助營收認列，及成熟製程漲價效應持續發酵(營收認列主要以晶圓代工
廠前⼀季營收為主，成熟製程主要自2Q26開始漲價)，推估營收13.2億元，QoQ+8.7%，EPS 9.45元。 
營運逐季成⻑將由2026年延續至2027年：展望未來，主要來自市場對先進製程技術、AI 相關應用、安全解決方
案的需求持續強勁，帶動授權⾦持續成⻑趨勢，及權利⾦成⻑動能可望加速，主要來自於新增先進製程應用帶來
的更高單價、新產品應用放量、PUF權利⾦貢獻擴大，以及具較高權利⾦的MTP產品占比提升，其中1)來自先進製
程貢獻持續發酵，由於先進製程單價更高，單⼀授權⾦單價數倍於平均，營收助益將更大，目前授權⾦NRE的佔
比，已經超過50%，未來權利⾦可望明顯提升，且3nm已有客戶量產成功，並持續朝2nm開發，未來對於權利⾦
貢獻將更顯著，因此3Q26無論是授權⾦或權利⾦同為新高或次高⽔準，未來將更具成⻑潛⼒；2)成熟製程回溫：
因AI周邊的外溢效果帶動晶圓代工廠產能利用率提升，自2Q26開始逐步漲價，而晶圓代工將持續漲價至2027年，
⼒旺自3Q26開始同步受惠，此外，既有應用升級帶動權利⾦單價提升(如16nm 折疊式手機OLED DDI，PMIC從
8吋轉到55nm)，對於營收結構仍以成熟製程為主的⼒旺將是⼀大福⾳；3)PUF自2Q25起，授權⾦及權利⾦貢獻
開始放大，其中大部分來自先進製程，隨著 AI 持續蓬勃發展，目前Security 並非主要考量，而Caliptra僅開始逐
步建立相關規範，但近期全球性資安事件層出不窮，硬體安全需求有望加速進展，此外，Hardware Root of Trust 
正從選配變成標配，PUF潛在營運驚人，隨著製程進入2nm，晶片設計與製造成本持續提高，客戶對面積、功耗、
可靠度以及符合安全標準的要求也更為嚴格，NeoFuse及PUF-based security IP的競爭優勢也更加明顯。整體而
言，2026年營運逐季持續成⻑並續創新高將延續至2027年。 
營運逐季創高至2027年，中⻑期成⻑無虞：公司地位居產業頂端，全球布局完善，先進製程貢獻推升與成熟製程
漲價同步推營運向上，此外，隨著Caliptra規範推升更多客戶導入公司產品，PUF有望成為營運主⼒，⻑期營運成
⻑無虞，近期股價因系統性風險浮現投資價值，投資建議維持買進，目標價3,550元。

3Q26 EPS 低於市場預期 14%，預期 4Q26 毛利率小幅回升 
嘉澤 3Q26 營收 99.58 億元，季增 4.1%/年增 18.2%，其中伺服器營收年增
55%，惟記憶體與 PCB 缺料壓抑 CPU Socket 拉貨，原物料成本維持高檔，
加上新平台導入慢於預期，毛利率季減 0.6 個百分點至 44.8%。薪資費用高
於預期以及業外淨收益亦較上季下降，EPS 為 17.06 元，低於本中心/市場
預期 11.0%/14.4%。考量缺料延續及新平台挹注有限，下修 4Q26 營收
5.8%至 103.89 億元，季增 4.3%/年增 12.2%，毛利率估季增 0.3 個百分點
至 45.1%，EPS 下修 12.6%至 18.79 元。 
QD 擴產及伺服器平台換代帶動 2027 年營收年增 30% 
QD 產能滿載，公司目標於未來一季將產能倍增，預估 QD 占總營收比重於
2026 年 12 月達 3–5%，2027 年全年接近 10%。AMD Venice 預計自 1Q27
末加速放量，2027 年平均採用率約 20%。Intel Oak Stream 預計於 1Q27
末至 2Q27 初導入，2H27 採用率估達 10–15%。新平台有助提升 CPU 
Socket 單價及 PCIe 6.0 連接器採用率，預估 2027 年伺服器營收年增 50%，
帶動整體營收年增 30%。 
維持買進及目標價 2,200 元 
伺服器缺料及 PC 業務疲弱使營收低於前估，原物料成本居高、新平台滲透
慢於預期，加上低於公司平均毛利率的 QD 占比提高及薪資費用增加，下修
2026/2027 年 EPS 6.0%/5.4%至 74.92/106.29 元。評價方面，本中心仍看
好 2027 年 QD 擴產將擴大營收與獲利貢獻，Venice 與 Oak Stream 逐步放
量則有助改善 CPU Socket 產品組合。隨營收規模擴大，預估 2027 年營益
率由 24.5%回升至 28.3%，帶動 EPS 年增 41.9%，因此維持買進評等，以 
2027 年預估 EPS 106.29 元 21 倍本益比推得目標價 2,200 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS 為 17.06 元，低於市場預期 14%，主因薪資費用提升以及
原物料成本居高、新平台導入進度不如預期，毛利率季減至 44.8%。 
⧫  
記憶體與 PCB 缺料持續壓抑 CPU Socket 拉貨，下修 4Q26 EPS 
12.6%至 18.79 元，預期毛利率小幅回升。 
⧫  
QD 放量與 AMD/Intel 新平台導入帶動 2027 年營收年增 30%、EPS
年增 42%至 106.29 元，維持買進及目標價 2,200 元。

營運分析
3Q26 毛利率低於預期，薪資費用提升，EPS 低於市場預期 14%
嘉澤 3Q26 營收 99.58 億元，季增 4.1%/年增 18.2%，低於本中心/市場預期 1.7%/2.5%，其中伺服
器營收年增 55%，占總營收 53.1%，惟記憶體與 PCB 缺料持續限制客戶出貨，CPU Socket 拉貨
低於預期。原物料成本維持高檔，伺服器新平台導入慢於預期，加上部分桌機客戶因缺料改採支援
DDR4 或 DDR3 的舊平台，使低單價、低毛利產品占比偏高，毛利率季減 0.6 個百分點至 44.8%，
低於本中心/市場預期 0.7/0.9 個百分點。中國廠區自 2Q26 起調薪，使費用率年增約 0.7 個百分點。
受惠於營收規模擴大，費用率仍季減 1.6 個百分點至 21.9%，營益率升至 22.9%，營業利益季增
8.6%至 22.84 億元。業外淨收益由上季 4.95 億元降至 0.95 億元，歸屬母公司淨利季減 3.3%至

19.19 億元。EPS 為 17.06 元，季減 3.5%/年減 17.6%，低於本中心/市場預期 11.0%/14.4%。

新平台導入速度不如預期，下修 4Q26 EPS 13%至 18.79 元
嘉澤 10 月營收 32.0 億元，月減 5%/年增 12%，達本中心 4Q26 營收原估的 29%。公司預期 4Q26
營收呈低個位數季增，零組件缺料仍是主要出貨限制，因此本中心下修 4Q26 營收 5.8%至 103.89
億元，季增 4.3%/年增 12.2%。SOCAMM 等新品出貨增加有助毛利率回升，惟 AMD Venice 年底
採用率僅估 3–5%，Intel Oak Stream 預計於 1Q27 末至 2Q27 初開始導入，高針腳數 CPU Socket

對 4Q26 產品組合的挹注低於前估，加上原物料成本維持高檔，以及毛利率低於公司平均的 QD 出
貨增加，本中心下修 4Q26 毛利率 1.4 個百分點至 45.1%，季增 0.3 個百分點。預估營業利益季增
10.2%至 25.17 億元，EPS 下修 12.6%至 18.79 元，季增約 10%/年減 16.5%。

QD 擴產與伺服器平台換代帶動 2027 年營收年增 30%
本中心預估嘉澤 2027 年營收年增 30%，主要成長動能來自 QD 放量與通用伺服器平台換代。公司
預估 2027 年伺服器整機出貨量年增 10–15%，新平台導入也將推升 CPU Socket 單價，並帶動
PCIe 6.0 連接器採用率提升。AMD Venice 目前採用率不到 3%，預計自 1Q27 末加速放量，2027
年全年採用率約 20%。Intel Oak Stream 預計於 1Q27 末至 2Q27 初開始導入，2H27 採用率估達

10–15%，營收貢獻主要落在下半年。隨 PCIe 連接器與線材市占率提升，本中心預估 2026/2027
年伺服器營收分別年增 56%/50%。嘉澤另預計於 2H27 開始出貨 Arm CPU 平台的客製化 Socket。
QD 目前產能滿載，公司目標於未來一季將產能倍增。預估 QD 占總營收比重於 2026 年 12 月達
3–5%，2027 年全年接近 10%。QD 放量將稀釋整體毛利率，但新增營業費用相對有限，規模擴大
後仍可挹注營業利益及 EPS。隨伺服器新平台採用率提高及 SOCAMM 出貨增加，本中心預估
2027 年毛利率由 46.2%回升至 48.5%。NPO Socket 預計於 2027 年以總頻寬 3.2Tbps 的產品小量
出貨，占營收不到 2%，2028 年隨單價較高的 6.4Tbps 產品導入，營收貢獻將更為明顯。
非伺服器業務方面，公司預估 2027 年 PC 市場出貨量再減約 10%。本中心估嘉澤 2026/2027 年筆
電營收分別年減 17%/4%，桌機營收年減 30%/年增 1%;策略客戶營收年增 3%/2%，車用年增

27%/15%，工業及醫療年增 22%/20%。嘉基受惠於高速線材出貨成長，營收預估年增 12%/40%。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第5 頁，共 16頁  
產業概況  
2026 年 PC 出貨恐衰退近雙位數 
記憶體價格大漲，影響 PC 出貨。受到上游記憶體成本大幅上揚，PC 廠商即使轉嫁或降低規格，
均將影響消費者購買意願，因此預估 2026 年 PC(NB+DT)出貨衰退 9.6%，NB 出貨亦年減 9.7%。
2027 年 PC 及 NB 出貨量才有機會回升低個位數。 
上半年消費者提前拉貨，下半年出貨待觀察。1H26 市場預期記憶體及儲存裝置未來大漲，因此客
戶有提前拉貨現象，IDC 統計全球 PC 出貨 679 萬台，季減 16%，但年增 3%，2Q26 起 PC 步入
衰退 5%，下半年更衰退至 15~20%。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第6 頁，共 16頁  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026/2027 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 95%/46%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic)、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國主要 CSP 資本支出將年增 95%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA 的天下，AWS、Google、Meta 
ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將採購
TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，2026/2027 年通用型伺服器預估出貨量將年增
10%/9%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP
等業者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中
將消耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第7 頁，共 16頁  
ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，年增
15%及 31%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用 GPU 算力需求仍維持
成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者，2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下，CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本，ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第8 頁，共 16頁
獲利調整與股票評價
下修 2026/2027 年 EPS 6.0%/5.4%，維持買進及目標價 2,200 元
伺 服 器 供 應 鏈 缺 料 持 續 壓 抑 CPU Socket 拉 貨 ， 筆 電 及 桌 機 營 收 亦 低 於 前 估 ， 本 中 心 下 修
2026/2027 年營收 2.0%/2.6%至 392.43/510.58 億元，分別年增 16.2%/30.1%。原物料成本維持高
檔，AMD 及 Intel 新平台採用率提升慢於預期，加上 QD 營收占比提高，使毛利率改善幅度低於前
估。本中心下修 2026/2027 年毛利率 0.5/0.6 個百分點至 46.2%/48.5%。費用方面，中國廠區調薪
推升固定薪資支出，公司預估 2027 年單季營業費用約 22–25 億元。考量薪資費用高於前估，下修
2026/2027 年 EPS 6.0%/5.4%至 74.92/106.29 元，分別年增 6.8%/41.9%。

評價方面，本中心仍看好 2027 年 QD 擴產將擴大營收與獲利貢獻，Venice 與 Oak Stream 逐步放
量則有助改善 CPU Socket 產品組合。隨營收規模擴大，預估 2027 年營益率由 24.5%回升至
28.3%，帶動 EPS 年增 41.9%，因此維持買進評等，以 2027 年預估 EPS 106.29 元 21 倍本益比
推得目標價 2,200 元。

公司簡介  
嘉澤成立於 1986 年，專門生產及設計 PCIe、DDR、SATA 等連接器及 USB 連接埠，公司擁有完整的生產流程，包含設計、
加工、成型、沖壓、電鍍及自動化組裝，可提供客製化需求及標準型產品。公司為全球前三大 Server Socket (CPU 插槽)供
應商，市佔率約為 30-35%，同業為鴻海集團(鴻騰精密)及安費諾等。該產業的進入門檻高，多數產品皆已申請專利，且公
司實驗室已通過 ISO 認證，可縮短測試時間，並降低測試費用。嘉澤產品為電腦、伺服器、消費性電子產品之各式連接器
及零組件，旗下有四大事業體，分別是 PC/NB、伺服器、子公司嘉基及其他業務(包括 Type-C 連接器)。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 嘉澤整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於中間偏前的
位置，在電子零組件行業中的公司中排名略領先於同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 嘉澤的整體曝險屬於中等水準，優於電子零組件行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的 ESG 議
題包含公司治理、商業道德與資源使用等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：嘉澤在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司遵循適當的 ESG 揭露，
表明對投資者和公眾的強烈責任感。其 ESG 相關問題由董事會或經營團隊負責，建議將這些整合到核心業務戰略中。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明：(1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

7717.TT 萊德光電-KY 
 太空資料中心不是夢

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
萊德光電股份有限公司成立於 2014 年 8 月，為光纖被動元件廠商。產品包括光纖通
訊與雷射元件、衛星雷射通訊元件、端面檢測儀與設備、智慧照明等。3Q26 萊德光
電-KY 營收組成為衛星雷射通訊被動元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它 27%。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 495 
【前次投資建議】無 
【大盤指數/股價】36033 / 462

➢ 衛星雷射通訊被動元件帶動 3Q26 營收、毛利大幅成長 
萊德光電-KY 3Q26 營收 3.4 億元(YoY+65.4%，QoQ+1.4%)，營收年
增主因為衛星雷射通訊被動元件營收由 3Q25 的 2 億元(營收比重 48%)，
年增 94% 至 3Q26 的 3.4 億元( 營收比重 57%) 。毛利率 68.6% 
(YoY+10.5ppt，QoQ+4.7ppt)，EPS 2.33 元。3Q26 萊德光電-KY 營收
組成為衛星雷射通訊被動元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它
27%。低軌衛星客戶訂單持續挹注下，預計 4Q26 營收季增 16.6%至 3.9
億元，預估 2026 年營收 13.2 億元(YoY+53.4%)，毛利率 64.1%，EPS 
10.06 元。 
➢ 2027 年 Starship 載運 V3 引領衛星 LCT 需求成長 
研究員推測 2027 年 SpaceX 發射火箭將由 Falcon 9 改為 Starship 為
主，發射次數約 100 次，載運 V3 數量約 6,000 顆，並預期 Blue Origin、
Amazon LEO 將藉由 Starship、ULA 載運 100 顆、300 顆衛星至太空
軌道，載運總數估達 6,400 顆(YoY+51.1%)。在衛星迭代上，SpaceX V3
新增 Direct-to-Call 功能，上行頻寬約 160 至 200Gbps，下行頻寬達
1Tbps，所需光放大器級數上升，帶動衛星雷射通訊被動元件用量由 3 個
提升至 6 個、9 個。目前公司新舊客戶訂單及研發需求正持續增加，假
設光纖雷射被動元件、智慧照明等營收年增 20%，端面檢測儀與製造設
備出貨 10 台。其中，衛星雷射通訊被動元件營收年增約 50%，營收比
重達 60％，預估 2027 年營收 18 億元(YoY+36.1%)，毛利率 64.3%，
EPS 15.35 元。 
➢ 首次評等給予區間操作，目標價 495 元 
萊德光電-KY 於 2021 年切入衛星雷射通訊領域後，目前在太空雷射元
件領域競爭對手甚少。現階段低軌衛星產業處於發展初期，2026 年至
2030 年衛星發射數量隨著火箭發射數量增加、運載能力增強而以雙位數
CAGR 成長，將逐年帶動萊德光電-KY 衛星雷射通訊被動元件等營收走
高。參考近期低軌衛星產業 Forward PE 評價落在 15X~40X 區間。以
2028 年 EPS 換算，目前為 23X，位於區間中緣，故首次評等給予區間
操作，目標價 495 元(2028F PE 25X)。

公司介紹 
萊德光電股份有限公司成立於 2014 年 8 月，為光纖被動元件廠商，主營運子公司為
設在美國西雅圖的 Lightel Technologies, Inc.。產品包括光纖通訊元件、光纖雷射元
件、衛星雷射通訊元件、光纖端面檢測儀與光纖元件製造設備、智慧照明等。3Q26 萊
德光電-KY 營收組成為衛星雷射通訊被動元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它
27%。 
圖 1：萊德光電-KY 營收組成

資料來源：國票投顧 
衛星雷射通訊被動元件帶動 3Q26 營收、毛利大幅成長 
萊德光電-KY 3Q26 營收 3.4 億元(YoY+65.4%，QoQ+1.4%)，營收年增主因為衛星
雷射通訊被動元件營收由 3Q25 的 2 億元(營收比重 48%)，年增 94%至 3Q26 的 3.4
億元(營收比重 57%)。毛利率 68.6%(YoY+10.5ppt，QoQ+4.7ppt)，年增 10.5ppt
中，約 4ppt 來自於美國海關退稅款，其餘 6.5ppt 則為高毛利之衛星雷射通訊產品營
收比重提升所帶動。EPS 2.33 元。3Q26 萊德光電-KY 營收組成為衛星雷射通訊被動
元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它 27%。儘管原本預計在 4Q26 投產之泰國
廠，遞延至 2Q27 開始小量出貨，但在低軌衛星客戶訂單持續挹注下，預計 4Q26 營
收季增 16.6%至 3.9 億元，預估 2026 年營收 13.2 億元(YoY+53.4%)，毛利率 64.1%，
EPS 10.06 元。 
2027 年 Starship 載運 V3 引領衛星 LCT 需求成長 
觀察 2025 年、2026 年(截至 11/20)SpaceX LEO 發射數量各為 3,233 顆、2,300 顆，
火箭發射次數為 170 次、127 次，多以 Falcon 9 運載為主。研究員推測 2027 年 
SpaceX 發射火箭將由 Falcon 9 改為 Starship 為主，發射次數約 100 次，載運 V3 數
量約 6,000 顆，並預期 Blue Origin、Amazon LEO 將藉由 Starship、ULA 載運 100
顆、300 顆衛星至太空軌道，載運總數估達 6,400 顆(YoY+51.1%)。在衛星迭代上，
SpaceX V3 新增 Direct-to-Call 功能，上行頻寬約 160 至 200Gbps，下行頻寬達
1Tbps，所需光放大器級數上升，帶動衛星雷射通訊被動元件用量由 3 個提升至 6 個、
9 個。目前公司新舊客戶訂單及研發需求正持續增加，假設光纖雷射被動元件、智慧
照明等營收年增 20%，端面檢測儀與製造設備出貨 10 台。其中，衛星雷射通訊被動
元件營收年增約 50%，營收比重達 60％，預估 2027 年營收 18 億元(YoY+36.1%)，
毛利率 64.3%，EPS 15.35 元。

2028 年建立太空資料中心，AIDC LEO 為成長動能 
展望 2028 年，SpaceX 可能於外太空建立約 5GW AI 運算能力，並在軌道部署約 1
萬個 Nvidia GPU 機架。目前 SpaceX 等低軌衛星廠商申請 AIDC 衛星數量已超過 100
萬顆。基於每顆 AIDC 衛星要與前後、左右衛星相連，需配置 6 顆 LCT，較現行衛星
增加 3 顆 LCT，而單顆 LCT 需要 6~９個光纖被動元件(包含合束器、隔離器、波長分
波多工器、濾波器、分光器與端帽等)，因此單顆 AIDC 衛星光纖被動元件用量由 18~27
個增至 36~54 個，帶動萊德光電-KY 產值增為原本的 2 倍至 3 倍。除了 AIDC 衛星
之外，地表的光基地台將以高功率雷射幫助低軌衛星傳遞訊號及能量，解決低軌衛星
太陽能板重量大、供給效率低的問題，並建置太空與地表連接的 6G 寬頻全光網路，
此亦是雷射被動元件營收成長動能。假設 2028 年 SpaceX Starship 發射次數達 130
次，V3 載運總數達 7,800 顆(YoY+21.9%)，光纖雷射被動元件、智慧照明等營收年增
15%至 20%，端面檢測儀與製造設備出貨維持 10 台，在泰國廠營收貢獻提升下，預
估 2028 年營收 23 億元(YoY+27.5%)，毛利率 64.3%，EPS 19.81 元。 
太空雷射對 LEO 發展不可或缺，首次評等給予區間操作，目標價 495 元 
萊德光電-KY 於 2021 年切入衛星雷射通訊領域後，目前在太空雷射元件領域競爭對
手甚少。現階段低軌衛星產業處於發展初期，2026 年至 2030 年衛星發射數量隨著火
箭發射數量增加、運載能力增強而以雙位數 CAGR 成長，將逐年帶動萊德光電-KY 衛
星雷射通訊被動元件等營收走高。參考近期低軌衛星產業 Forward PE 評價落在
15X~40X 區間。以 2028 年 EPS 換算，目前為 23X，位於區間中緣，故首次評等給
予區間操作，目標價 495 元(2028F PE 25X)。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 비현금성 비용 항목 개선으로 시장 기대치
를 상회했으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인플레이션 등으로 연간 가이던스는 
하향되었습니다. 올해 생산 차질에 따라 이연된 신차 판매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시화하는 부분에 주목 필요합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제외하면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년으로 이연된 것이라는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본격화된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로컬업체들의 공세를 방어하는 모습을 
확인하는 것이 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 노후차종 중심 운영에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근원적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

## Document 232: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입한화에어로스페이스(012450) 일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에_b94e5eb0.pdf

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450/KS) 
일시적 부진, 4Q부터 성장 가도에 재진입 
2026-10-30

조선/방산. 한승한, RA. 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 1,300,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 760,000 원 
상승여력: 71.1%

Signal: 3Q26, 수출 물량 저조 및 국내 낮은 마진 물량 인식으로 어닝 쇼크 
Key: 4Q 부터 내년까지 수출 물량 빠르게 회복하며 실적 성장 이어갈 전망 
Step: 연내 새로운 중장기 성장동력이 될 중동 수주계약 기대. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 수출 물량 납품 저조로 어닝 쇼크  
3Q26 연결 매출액 6 조 943 억원(-6.0% YoY, -34.4% QoQ), 영업이익 4,520 억원
(-46.2% YoY, -66.9% QoQ)으로, 시장예상치(1 조 1,615억원)를 대폭 하회하는 어닝 쇼
크 기록했다. ①폴란드 중심의 수출 물량의 매출 인식 시점이 4 분기로 이연되며, 수출 물량 
비중이 감소한 점이 컸다. ②내수 물량 또한 감소했으며, 특히 국내 물량 중에서도 상대적
으로 마진율 낮은 유무인 무기체계 초도 물량이 이번 분기에 집중됐다. ③내수&수출 전반
적인 지상방산 매출 비중 감소에 따른 고정비 부담이 컸으며, ④일회성 요인으로 대전 사업
장 사고에 따른 복구 비용 약 300 억원과 충당금 및 기타 비용 총 160 억원, 그리고 환율 
하락으로 영업이익의 약 10% 수준의 감소 효과 발생했다. 다만 4Q26 부터 이번 분기 이
연된 폴란드 중심의 수출 물량 인식이 반영되면서 큰 폭의 탑라인 및 이익 성장 시현할 전
망이며, 대전 사업장 또한 내년 상반기 가동 재개 예상됨에 따라 지상방산 부문은 전년 대비 
성장 폭 두드러지며 사측 가이던스 15% 매출 성장 충분히 달성 가능할 전망이다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 130 만원으로 하향 
한화에어로스페이스에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가 기존 160 만원에서 130
만원으로 하향한다. 최근 Peer 그룹의 주가 하락에 따라 멀티플 하향 조정했기 때문이다. 
폴란드 천무 유도탄 JV 에 이어 현지화가 전제된 K9 3차 계약을 추진 중이며, 국내 장약 생
산능력 증설을 통한 유럽향 수요 대응과 미국 내 생산거점 구축까지 진행 중에 있다. 또한 
미국 차륜형 자주포 사업 시제 선정에 이은 궤도형 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다. 중동 국
가들의 방공무기체계에 대한 계약 논의는 매우 가시적인 상황이며, 사우디 MNG 사업 수주 
또한 시점의 문제일 뿐이다. 기존 유럽에 더해 중동은 현지화 전략을 기반으로 동사의 새로
운 중장기 성장동력이 될 지역이며, 올해 4 분기부터 해당 수주 모멘텀과 함께 큰 폭의 실적 
성장이 맞물리며 리레이팅 가능할 전망이다. 선제적 매수 기회라는 판단이다.

작성자(한승한)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

## Document 241: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

## Document 283: Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Blue Wave: Democrats 
sweep the US midterms  
Summary 
Event: The Democrats gained control of the House of 
Representatives and the Senate at the US midterm elections 
that took place this week.  
Our View: With the US government now no longer under 
unified control of the Republicans, gridlock has returned to 
Washington. We believe investors should not overreact to 
these results and should stay invested. 
In equities, investors should continue to diversify their 
equity exposure into more defensive sectors. As we 
transition to mid-cycle for the equity markets, we expect 
stocks to continue to deliver positive returns in the 8-10% 
range per annum, a downshift from the torrid pace of gains in 
the earlier part of the cycle.  
In bonds, income remains attractive given recent spread 
widening. We favour non-hyperscaler investment grade 
corporate bonds at the three to seven-year part of the 
curve. We continue to expect the US dollar to remain range-
bound in the near term, as we expect two more rate hikes 
from the Fed, but as this mini rate hike cycle ends in the 
second quarter of 2027, the USD index (DXY) is likely to 
depreciate towards 98.5 as markets start to anticipate a Fed 
rate cut in the second half of next year.  
Oil is likely to rebound in the short-term on heightened 
geopolitical risk, but as supply chains adapt to the current 
situation and Congress exercises oversight on defense 
spending, WTI prices are likely to drift towards our 12-month 
target of USD 80 per barrel.

Democrats made solid gains across the Senate, House 
of Representatives and State Governors 
Results reported as at 7:45PM Singapore Time

Source: The Wall Street Journal, Standard Chartered

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

Major implications of Democrat control of 
Congress   
1) Fiscal expansionary policies may slow. Democrats are 
now in position to block any new tax cuts or spending plans 
from the Trump administration. At the same time, any fiscal 
policies proposed by Democrats may also face Trump’s veto. 
Hence the fiscal trajectory of the US will likely remain stuck 
over the next two years. As we get into the second half of 
2027, the US debt ceiling will once again loom, and gridlock 
could potentially bring back government shutdowns. 
However, as they have always done in the past, compromise 
will ultimately prevail, in our opinion.  
2) Trump remains in control of foreign policy. With the 
midterms now out of the way, could Trump decide to re-start 
combat operations with Iran? We believe there is a risk that 
US and Israel – now that both countries’ have concluded 
important elections – will once again be tempted to restart 
combat operations to force an end to the conflict. However, 
the duration of any renewed hostilities may be limited by 
Congress next year as the Democrats will likely withhold 
funding for further military operations. In the near-term, oil 
prices may experience renewed volatility before stabilising at 
a lower level next year. The introduction of new tariffs on 
trading partners may prove harder with Democrat oversight. 
3) AI data centre moratoriums could grow, though model 
development regulatory changes would be harder to 
pass. While the headlines have focused on the House and 
Senate results, one important development has also 
occurred at the gubernatorial level. Notably, Democrats have 
won the governor races in Texas and Georgia, two big data 
centre states. As data centre build out permits are largely 
decided at the local state level, this will have important 
ramifications for build out plans next year. However, any 
Democratic proposals to regulate AI model developments 
would need to be signed off by President Trump, who 
continues to wield veto power on any such proposals.   
Asset class strategy 
Equities: Remain invested and continue to diversify.  
While investors initially overreacted, President Trump’s 
relatively conciliatory response has provided some welcome 
relief, helping markets recover strongly from their pre-market 
lows. Hence, we recommend investors avoid 
overreacting, remain invested and continue to diversify 
their equity exposure across sectors and regions.  
A few themes stand out from the early pre-market reaction to 
the mid-term elections. On the positive side, defensive stocks 
continue to fare well, while themes favoured by Democrats 
are also outperforming. Clean energy is a notable 
beneficiary, with solar stocks among the biggest winners

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation cycle 
is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle transition, 
where equity returns moderate but remain positive

before the newly elected representatives are sworn in and 
take control of the House and Senate? As we have been 
highlighting in recent notes, equity markets should still deliver 
positive returns, albeit at a more moderate pace. Hence, we 
recommend staying invested but diversification remains key 
to navigating the next phase of the cycle.

while CCCs have widened to distressed levels. For income-

## Document 296: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause, however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downside 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widely 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is 
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals”. By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutinised. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike. But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, the decision is justified by the need to further analyse risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that any rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United States). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the HICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1%-2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

SOVEREIGN DEBT: WHEN SHORT-TERM SHOCKS AND SHIFTS IN DEBT STRUCTURE LEAVE A LASTING FISCAL
BURDEN — THE CASE OF BRAZIL
Salim Hammad
Brazil’s recent post-election yield shock risks leaving a lasting imprint on public finances — compounded by already high interest rates and a
changing debt structure. Large refinancing needs and a rising share of floating-rate debt will transmit rapidly and significantly higher interest

rates to the debt service through distinct but reinforcing channels.
SOURCES : MACROBOND, BCB

An abrupt post-election market awakening

In the wake of the 2nd round of the general elections in late October, Brazilian markets — which had expected a Flavio Bolsonaro
victory — sold off sharply after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. Both the equity and FX markets
experienced large intraday drops (14% of the Ibovespa stock index and -11% BRL vs. USD). Meanwhile, the yield on Brazil’s 10-year
government bond rose 250 basis points to 15.5%, reaching its highest level in over a decade (Chart 1) as investors worried about

the fiscal impact of costly campaign promises amidst large and persistent fiscal imbalances.
Fiscal risks have reshaped Brazil’s public debt structure, magnifying the cost of persistently high interest rates
Absent a confidence-infusing fiscal adjustment, Brazil’s interest bill is unlikely to decline in the short term, maintaining upward
pressure on public debt (83% of GDP in October 2026) and limiting budgetary leeway.
Three transmission channels, amplified by changing features of the debt structure, explain this persistence.

The first channel: The rise in long-term yields will increase rollover costs.
The sovereign must refinance roughly 20% of its debt stock each year. An average maturity of 4.1 years spreads refinancing over
time but does not shield the interest bill from a sustained rise in borrowing costs. As maturing securities are replaced, higher
issuance rates progressively lift the average borrowing cost — even for fixed-rate debt, whose interest costs remain unchanged

until refinancing. A sustained yield shock therefore feeds into debt servicing as the stock rolls over.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 10 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The second channel: Demand for floating-rate instruments will strengthen until confidence in fiscal policy improves.
In turn, a larger share of SELIC-linked debt heightens the debt stock’s sensitivity to the prevailing high policy rate. Already SELIC-linked
securities reached a record 52.6% of the debt stock in August 2026, around 12 percentage points above their share at the start of President

Lula’s last term, versus a low of 18.4% in September 2014 (Chart 2). Weaker demand for fixed-rate securities with maturities exceeding five
years, amid increased fiscal uncertainty, has encouraged this shift1.
The third channel: The BCB may delay rate cuts until fiscal policy over the next mandate is better defined.
A slower easing cycle would prolong high interest costs on SELIC-linked debt, whose servicing costs adjust with the policy rate without

waiting for refinancing. With these securities accounting for a growing share of the debt stock, delayed monetary easing would postpone
relief for the Treasury’s interest bill — even if long-term market yields subsequently ease.
These channels operate at different speeds: a rise in long-term yields increases rollover costs, whereas floating-rate debt reprices with
the SELIC rate, not automatically with 10-year yields. Meanwhile, a slower monetary easing cycle and stronger demand for floating-rate

securities compound pressure on the interest bill.
The fiscal–monetary policy nexus is therefore likely to remain a source of strain on Brazil’s public finances: fiscal uncertainty could delay
monetary easing, while greater floating-rate exposure would prolong the burden of high policy rates on the interest bill.
salim.hammad@bnpparibas.com
1 Fiscal slippage and uncertainty make investors less willing to lock in nominal returns over long horizons: if inflation or market yields rise, the purchasing power of fixed payments

declines or the bonds’ market value falls. Investors therefore demand higher yields to bear this risk, making long-term fixed-rate funding more expensive for the Treasury. By issuing
floating-rate bonds instead, the Treasury offers returns that adjust with the SELIC rate, sustaining demand, securing financing and reducing the risk of failed auctions. However
longer floating-rate maturities may reduce rollover risk, but do not remove the repricing exposure. Floating-rate issuance helps secure financing but transfers interest-rate risk to

the Treasury.

## Document 407: The ECB’s new team faces a testing start — with the skills to match (2026-11-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The ECB’s new team faces a testing start — with the skills to match (2026-11-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026EDITORIAL
THE ECB’S NEW TEAM FACES A TESTING START — WITH THE SKILLS TO MATCH
European leaders are set to agree on a package deal at their 26–27 November meeting for the three ECB Board seats falling
vacant in the first five months of 2027. Individually, they differ markedly from their predecessors. Collectively, they bring
a remarkably similar mix of skills. Policy preferences may be another matter. In any case, they will need to hit the ground

running. Top priorities: communications, crisis management, soft landing, balance sheet, policy-making upgrades, and role
of the euro.
MORE CONTINUITY THAN MEETS THE EYES, WITH A BONUS
Based on the nominations filed by last Friday’s deadline, EU leaders
are expected to appoint Spain’s Pablo Hernández de Cos to replace
Christine Lagarde as President in February; France’s Laurence Boone
to take over market operations from Isabel Schnabel in January; and

Germany’s Markus Brunnermeier to succeed Philip Lane as chief eco-
nomist in May.
The incoming Board members share their predecessors’ international
standing and commitment to Europe and the euro. Beyond that, the in-
dividual differences are striking. Yet together they offer the same blend
of monetary policy expertise, academic financial economics and politi-
cal nous. Monetary experience comes not from the chief economist this
time, but from Hernandez de Cos, a former Bank of Spain governor who

now heads the Bank for International Settlements, the “bank of central
banks”. Brunnermeier brings his expertise as a Princeton professor;
Boone her experience as a presidential “sherpa”, OECD chief economist
and minister for Europe. Her stints at Axa and several international
investment banks also bring an insider’s knowledge of capital markets
that the Board has lacked since Mario Draghi. All three were involved,
if not in frontline roles, in managing the Eurozone crises of the past

decade. The essential skills are there.
WILL THE ECB LEAN MORE DOVISH?
A more dovish ECB is not a foregone conclusion. Judging by his record,
I expect the new President to seek the same “owl” label as his prede-
cessor: neither dove nor hawk. Boone is likely to be as dovish as Lane.
Brunnermeier, however, is hardly a hawk — a marked contrast with the
outgoing German Board member, Schnabel. The Governing Council’s
hawks will need another champion. They will surely find at least one:

perhaps Vice-President Boris Vujcic, who succeeded Luis de Guindos
last June, or a new national central bank governor. Nature abhors a
vacuum, in ornithology as elsewhere.
NO SMOOTH SAILING AHEAD FOR THE NEW CREW
Thorny challenges await:
First, inflation is running at twice the ECB’s target, and has probably
not peaked, even if oil prices go no higher. Growth has proved sur-
prisingly resilient to rate rises, so the ever-feared trade-off between
fighting inflation and sustaining growth has yet to materialise. It will

sharpen if inflation proves stickier than expected.
Second, markets have been unsettled for weeks by fears of an AI
bubble, and of an ever-ascending path for French public debt, which
the coming spring elections could worsen. The risk premium on French
debt has hovered around twice its level at the start of the year, threate-
ning contagion to other high debt Eurozone sovereigns, notably Italy,
Belgium, and Spain. Market moves have remained orderly, and finan-

cial conditions have tightened only moderately thus far. But that may
not last.
Third, cyber disruptions, whether driven by AI agents or other sources,
have become alarmingly more frequent in recent weeks. They could
rattle the financial system, in the Eurozone or globally, at any time.
THE NEW LEADERSHIP’S PRIORITIES
The first few days
Find a sound communications footing. Many a central bank leader ce-
lebrated as a tough act to follow upon leaving office caused unintended

market volatility early in their tenure. Little prepares for the power of
every word to move markets, or for an off-the-cuff remark to undo the
effects of a carefully drafted statement. Sometimes, the lessons can be
learnt without serious consequences. Now is not such a time.
The first few weeks
- Build a new modus operandi. Each of Hernández de Cos’s prede-
cessors brought a different leadership style and skill set, and faced
different personal dynamics on the Board and Governing Council. Each

eventually made it work. That cannot be taken for granted. Efficient, ef-
fective, and well-understood decision-making — for which the current
ECB deserves high marks — takes work. With two-thirds of the Board
changing in less than a year, establishing an effective way of working
will be paramount. Fortunately, Hernandez de Cos wins high praise
from those involved in the many central banking committees he has
chaired over the years.
- Take ownership of crisis management playbooks — before a crisis

hits. Several, no doubt, exist already. The new President and Board
members will want to be comfortable with them and may add their
own touches. No crisis follows a script, but thinking through the op-
tions, trade-offs, and contingencies ahead of time can save precious
time and avert mistakes.
The first few months
- Deliver a soft landing. Both the ECB’s central forecast and econo-
mists’ consensus envisage Eurozone inflation returning close to target

by late 2027 or early 2028, with growth holding at 1–1.5%. The risks
outlined above mean that this outcome is far from assured. The ECB
will need to stay nimble.
- Finalise the balance sheet strategy. How large should the ECB’s ba-
lance sheet be in steady state, and which instruments should manage
systemic liquidity? The 2025 Strategy Review left these questions open:
there was broad agreement that unwinding crisis-era asset purchases
could stay on autopilot for a while. That phase is nearing its end. Better

to have a plan before problems arise, not least given the fraught mar-
ket environment that has settled in.

### Page 2

4
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
- Consider the combined effect of monetary, balance sheet and macro-
prudential policies. A coherent, comprehensive approach would be a
major step forward and improve the odds of a soft landing. The new
Board is uniquely qualified to take it: for the first time, it will have deep
expertise and experience across all these dimensions, starting with the
President and Vice-President.

Longer term
- Understand AI’s economic effects on aggregate demand, labour mar-
kets and productivity, and the implications for monetary policy. Ex-
cellent work on this is already under way, but the effects will evolve
rapidly. Unless policymakers anticipate them, or at least understand
them in real time, monetary policy risks missing the mark. The ECB
must also clarify which effects monetary policy can address, and which
belong with fiscal authorities, such as a rise in structural unemploy-

ment, were it to occur.
- Use AI to improve monetary policy analysis and decision-making.
Brunnermeier has been at the forefront of academic economists’ work
in this area, and should be able to play a decisive role in upgrading the
ECB’s work in this regard.
- Continue to promote the euro’s international role and its place as a
pillar of EU sovereignty. That means continuing to: advocate for pro-
gress in the architecture of the European Monetary Union; modernise

euro-based cross-border payments and remain at the frontier of sup-
port for a vibrant euro-based blockchain finance ecosystem through
the ECB’s Pontes and Appia projects.
I am confident that the new leadership is as qualified as any to deliver.
Nonetheless, their task is a daunting one. All EU citizens and corpo-
rates should wish them every success. I certainly do.
Isabelle Mateos y Lago
isabelle.mateosylago@bnpparibas.com

Standard Chartered Bank

Charts of the week: Downbeat investor positioning, sentiment

Investor positioning and sentiment indicators suggest limited further downside in US equities  
US equity combined investor positioning trend and S&P500 3m forward returns Our Fear and Greed index

Source: Vanda, Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

Editorial 
Upgrading equities, downgrading gold  
Strategy summary: US equity volatility has returned to levels 
previously seen during last year’s Liberation Day tariffs. Three 
factors drove the spike: uncertainty over AI spending after the 
Democrats’ landslide mid-term victory, Nvidia CEO Jensen 
Huang’s abrupt departure and reinstatement less than two 
weeks later, and concern that reaccelerating inflation could 
prompt a more aggressive Fed response. 
The sharp downturn in US equities from their October record 
high has taken valuations back to 2022 bear market levels. Our 
quantitative models still signal caution, but investor positioning 
and sentiment suggest the market is oversold. Having stayed 
Neutral through the sell-off, we are raising global equities to 
Overweight as valuations improve. President Trump’s 
intervention at Nvidia reinforces our view that AI spending 
remains central to the US growth outlook. We are also cutting 
gold to a Neutral allocation in our balanced foundation portfolio. 
Upgrading global equities to Overweight: We cut global 
equities to Neutral in our foundation asset allocation in late 
October, because market breadth was narrowing and we 
expected volatility around the US mid-terms. That move helped 
cushion the portfolio against the subsequent sell-off. Despite 
the Democrats’ landslide, we still expect AI spending to grow, 
though more slowly than over the past three years. Our forecast 
for AI capex remains below consensus, but we think the sell-off 
prices in a sharper spending cut than is warranted (page 4). 
Criticality of AI capex to US growth. President Trump’s 
intervention in Nvidia’s leadership underscores the importance 
of AI investment to US growth and competitiveness with China. 
S&P500 valuations are now 11% below their 10-year average, 
2027 earnings growth outlook remains resilient, while sentiment 
and positioning suggest strong 3-month forward returns (see 
chart). That combination supports a return to Overweight in 
global equities. We remain Overweight US and Asia ex-Japan 
equities, which we see as the main beneficiaries of AI spending. 
Near-term risk from Congress in transition, divided Fed: 
Volatility is likely to stay elevated as control of Congress shifts 
from Republicans to Democrats. The next flashpoint is 11 
December, when government funding expires. Defence 
spending and the Iran conflict are the main sticking points. We 
expect a continuing resolution to keep the government funded 
temporarily, leaving longer-term decisions until after 3 January, 
when Democrats take control of both chambers. 
Tariffs add uncertainty. The latest US tariff proposal against 
trade partners, if it survives a potential challenge by the new 
Democrat-led Congress, will lift the average tariff rate by 2.5ppt 
to c. 14%. That will take the rate back to February levels when 
the Supreme Court struck down prior tariffs, but well below the 
2025 shock. The tariff rates align with what has been agreed in 
trade deals, but they are likely to marginally fuel US inflation. 
Disinflation, equity volatility to challenge Fed hawks. We 
expect the Fed to raise rates for the second time this cycle on 
9 December after stronger-than-expected inflation and jobs 
reports, then hike once more in Q1 2027 as near-term inflation 
remains elevated. However, US crude oil and gasoline prices 
have fallen 20% from their October highs, and oil supply could 
exceed demand in Q1 as Middle East output returns to pre-
conflict levels. We expect inflation to ease by mid-2027. Along 
with tighter financial conditions following the equity sell-off, that 
could make Fed hawks less inclined to tighten and pave the 
way for rate cut in the second half of 2027. Equities should 
benefit as markets price out further tightening. 
Downgrading gold to Neutral: Alongside the equity upgrade, 
we are cutting gold from Overweight to Neutral, reducing its 
weight in our balanced allocation from 6% to 5%. Last week, 
we lowered our three-month and 12-month gold price forecasts 
to USD 4,000/oz and USD 4,500/oz, respectively. A resilient 
dollar, expectations of higher Fed rates and liquidity needs amid 
the equity selloff have weighed on gold this year (see page 4). 
Opportunity for those under-invested in gold. Gold’s long-
term case remains intact. Fiscal concerns and geopolitical risks 
are likely to sustain Emerging Market (EM) central bank buying. 
Gold is likely to remain volatile as markets reassess the Fed 
outlook. Still, investors with a long-term horizon who are below 
their target weight can use the recent sell-off to average in.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 27 November 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the drivers behind your decision to upgrade 
equities to Overweight and downgrade gold to Neutral? 
Our view: We upgrade global equities to Overweight from a 
Neutral allocation. The risks now appear priced in, while 
earnings growth remains robust for a market in a mid-cycle 
phase. This improved risk-reward strengthens our relative 
preference for equities, and despite near-term volatility, 
underpins our decision to downgrade gold to a Neutral 
allocation. While we remain constructive on the precious 
metal's recovery towards USD 4,000/oz, our latest stance on it 
reflects a more measured allocation. 
Rationale: We downgraded global equities to a Neutral allocation in 
our Global Market Outlook published on 30 October 2026. At the 
time, we expected higher near-term volatility in an equity market 
transition to a mid-cycle bull market. However, the recent sell-off 
has gone further than we expected, with much of the risks now 
priced in. The sell-off has been centred on the AI theme. While we 
acknowledge downside risk to AI capex estimates, this risk is more 
than priced in by markets, in our view.  
Global equity valuations have fallen to a 14.6x 12-month forward 
price-to-earnings (P/E) ratio, a level last seen in the 2022 bear 
market. The consensus 2027 earnings growth estimate remains 
robust at 16%, which we expect to largely materialise. We favour 
broad diversification across sectors, with an Overweight stance on 
the US IT, US healthcare, US financial, US materials, Europe ex-
UK financial and Japan financial sectors. Regionally, we remain 
Overweight US and Asia ex-Japan equities, which are expected 
to offer the highest earnings growth in 2027. 
In contrast, near-term headwinds drive our decision to downgrade 
gold to Neutral, as the metal has faced pressure from a resilient 
USD, hawkish Fed expectations and liquidation to meet margin calls 
during the equity drawdown. However, fiscal concerns and continued 
demand from EM central banks support its longer-term appeal.  
Substantial Fed tightening is already priced into rates markets. 
Moderating inflation, helped by sustained lower oil prices, could 
allow the central bank to signal an earlier or lower rate peak than 
markets expect. This would ease upward pressure on US bond 
yields and soften the USD, supporting gold's recovery.  
In the near term, strong US data could sustain USD strength and 
prolong gold's volatility. We see firm support around USD 
3,300/oz, while a sustained break above USD 3,650/oz would 
strengthen our conviction in a recovery towards USD 4,000/oz. Our 
Neutral stance on gold, therefore, balances its recovery potential 
with near-term volatility and stronger expected returns from equities. 
We retain gold as a Neutral allocation, with a 5% weight in our 
balanced portfolio, maintaining exposure to its recovery while 
favouring equities on a relative basis. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

risk-reward following the recent sell-off

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What would signal that US tech equities are stabilising? 
Our view: Key indicators include steadier US government bond 
yields, confirmation that major cloud providers can fund their 
AI spending plans and evidence that customers are paying for 
AI services at scale. We favour staying invested and selectively 
adding exposure through our latest opportunistic equity ideas, 
such as US IT and US Mega Caps. The recent sell-off appears to 
have priced in a steeper cut to AI spending than we expected. 
We prefer companies with strong free cash flow generation and 
volume-led earnings growth rather than price-driven gains. 
Rationale: Steadier US government bond yields would ease 
pressure on tech valuations. On earnings, the next test is whether 
cloud providers can reaffirm their capex guidance and show that 
rising AI usage is translating into revenue. Longer-term customer 
commitments would give us greater confidence that demand can 
hold up beyond 2027. 
Our analysis suggests semiconductor valuations imply around 
USD 900bn of global AI capex in 2027 versus our forecast of USD 
1.2trn and consensus estimate of USD 1.4trn. Although we expect 
near-term volatility to remain elevated, long-term investors who have 
remained on the sidelines can gradually rebuild exposure while 
continuing to diversify across sectors and regions to manage 
concentration risk. 
—  Robin Xie, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Is now a good time to add to Japanese bank equities? 
Our view: Yes, Japanese banks remain one of our opportunistic 
equity ideas, and we favour adding exposure after their recent 
results. Wider lending spreads and firm corporate borrowing 
support earnings, while higher dividends and new buybacks 
return more capital to shareholders. We prefer banks with 
strong deposit franchises as interest rates rise. 
Rationale: All three Japanese megabanks beat September-quarter 
net profit expectations and raised their FY Mar-27 profit targets by 4-
14%. Consensus earnings estimates for the TOPIX Banks Index 
have since risen by 3-5%. Companies continued to borrow for 
investment and acquisitions between March and September, while 
non-performing loan ratios fell at each bank. 
We expect three further 25bps BoJ rate hikes by mid-2027, taking 
the policy rate from 1.25% to 2%. Rate hikes before the March year-
end could lift earnings above guidance, with a fuller benefit in FY 
Mar-28 as loans reprice. Faster increases in deposit costs or 
weaker loan demand could temper that upside. 
—  Robin Xie, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

We see downside risk of USD 200bn per annum to

pricing in a USD 500bn shortfall against

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 27 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How does the recent US and European macro data impact 
your rates and credit strategy? 
Our view: We continue to expect 25bps rate hikes from both the 
Fed and the ECB in December. Given the upside risk to yields, 
we avoid long-duration USD bonds, favouring the 3-7-year USD 
area. Meanwhile, we maintain a neutral stance on German 
Bunds and EUR credit ahead of the April 2027 French 
presidential election. 
Rationale: The reacceleration in US personal consumption 
expenditures (PCE) inflation in October validates the Fed’s hawkish 
stance following its 25bps rate hike in September. While rhetoric 
points to a potential 50bps move in December after the October hold, 
our base case continues to project a 25bps hike in December, 
as November’s retreat in fuel costs is expected to temper headline 
pressures. Given the risk of higher yields, we remain cautious on 
long-duration USD bonds. Conversely, resilient spreads and 
muted primary issuance support our preference for USD credit 
over rates, particularly in the 3-7-year segment.   
Euro area inflation is similarly re-accelerating above the ECB’s 2% 
medium-term target, while resilient economic activity data 
removes any near-term case for ECB easing, reinforcing our forecast 
for one 25bps rate hike in December. With the Germany 10-year 
bond (Bund) yield trading within our three-month target corridor of 
3.25-3.50% and French political instability driving defensive flows, 
we maintain a neutral stance on Bunds and EUR credit ahead of 
the April 2027 French presidential election. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  How will Australia's October inflation and Q3 2026 capex 
data impact the AUD? 
Our view: Firmer October inflation and stronger Q3 private new 
capex data strengthen the case for AUD stabilisation. We 
remain constructive over the next 12 months, but expect 
AUD/USD to remain rangebound between 0.65 and 0.69 in the 
near term, with global equity volatility restraining gains. 
Rationale: Australia’s October trimmed mean inflation of 3.8% y/y 
and the Q3 capex surge of +12.2% reinforce the case for the RBA 
to hold rates at 4.85% and challenge expectations of early easing.  
Further RBA policy tightening remains a risk if inflation persists, 
although weaker employment warrants caution. Capex strength also 
remains concentrated in data centres, limiting evidence of a broad 
recovery. These developments temper one source of AUD 
weakness. A sustained recovery still requires more favourable US 
rate expectations and global risk sentiment. Our expectation of a Fed 
rate peak by March 2027, followed by easing in late 2027, supports 
AUD/USD’s recovery towards our medium-term target of 0.74. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 27 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Could a recovery in risk sentiment weaken the Swiss franc 
(CHF) and the Japanese yen (JPY) in the near term? 
Our view: Yes, but only modestly for now. We expect CHF and 
JPY to retain near-term support as equity volatility persists. A 
sustained improvement in risk sentiment would be needed to 
drive more meaningful weakness in both currencies. 
Rationale: While improving risk sentiment would ultimately reduce 
safe-haven demand for CHF and JPY, we do not think this transition 
is complete yet. Equity volatility remains elevated following the 
recent AI-led sell-off, keeping markets vulnerable to renewed bouts 
of risk aversion. This should preserve haven demand and limit 
near-term depreciation in both currencies. 
For the JPY, persistent equity volatility should provide support 
by discouraging carry trades and restraining risk-seeking 
capital outflows. Meanwhile, USD/JPY approaching 160 increases 
intervention concerns, which should limit the extent of further JPY 
weakness. Expectations for further BoJ normalisation also offer an 
underlying policy backstop.  
We expect USD/JPY to test support towards 156. For the CHF, 
lingering market uncertainty should similarly sustain haven demand. 
While the CHF typically weakens in risk-on environments, it has 
historically underperformed the JPY during acute risk-off episodes. 
We expect JPY to outperform CHF as risk sentiment improves, 
capping CHF/JPY upside at around 193.7. 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

## Document 508: 世界黄金协会与投资机构共议黄金：利率定价回归，信用逻辑仍在

Source PDF: all report pdf/世界黄金协会与投资机构共议黄金：利率定价回归，信用逻辑仍在.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

今年国际⾦价⼀度较⾼点回撤超过30%，让市场重新审视⻩⾦的定价逻辑与配置价值。 11
⽉26⽇，世界⻩...
摘要：
今年国际⾦价⼀度较⾼点回撤超过30%，让市场重新审视⻩⾦的定价逻辑与配置价值。
11⽉26⽇，世界⻩⾦协会举办资产配置媒体分享会，并发布基于中国市场20余年历史数据的⻩
⾦配置研究。
世界⻩⾦协会此次研究显⽰，在特定股债现⾦组合中，⻩⾦的⻓期配置参考⽐例约为16%。在
经济放缓或滞胀时期，部分研究情景下的配置⽐例可提⾼⾄21%⾄28%；⽽在经济复苏或繁荣
时期，则可降⾄5%⾄11%，以更多保留组合的上⾏收益。
在实际情况中，机构配置还受到产品投资范围、⽐例限制及考核周期等因素的约束。
世界⻩⾦协会亚太区（除印度）研究负责⼈兼中国区⾏业拓展副总监贾舒畅指出，本轮⾦价回
撤过程中，不同类型投资者的仓位变化呈现出明显差异。
11⽉前三周，⻩⾦ETF出现资⾦流出，但9⽉及10⽉仍录得流⼊；相⽐之下，部分期货投资者及
基⾦经理⼤幅减少净多头仓位，期权市场也明显转向看跌。
亚洲与美国交易时段的⾦价表现亦出现分化。中国相对较低的利率环境，使⻩⾦仍具有⼀定的

配置吸引⼒；⽽美国利率上⾏及股市的投资机会，则提⾼了当地投资者持有⻩⾦的机会成本。
世界⻩⾦协会此前发布的市场评论指出，市场对美联储利率路径预期的重新定价、投机性持仓
收缩及资产配置轮动，是10⽉⾦价下跌的主要影响因素。
2007年⾄2022年，美国实际利率与⾦价保持较强的负相关关系。但2022年之后，新兴市场央⾏
及其他投资者的⻩⾦需求增⻓，在⼀定程度上抵消了实际利率上⾏对⾦价的压制。
今年，地缘政治冲突推升油价及通胀预期，利率因素重新主导阶段性⾏情，但央⾏购⾦需求仍
保持强劲。
世界⻩⾦协会数据显⽰，今年三季度，全球央⾏及其他官⽅机构净购⾦321吨，同⽐增⻓42%；
前三季度累计净购⾦675吨，同⽐增⻓6%。
阳光资产全球策略部负责⼈⽩雪⽯认为，两次地缘政治冲突引发的⻛险性质并不相同。2022年
市场更多关注资产安全及主权信⽤⻛险，⽽今年市场⾯临的更多是流动性冲击，⻩⾦反⽽可能
成为投资者筹措资⾦的变现⼯具。
与此同时，AI投资热潮对⻩⾦市场的影响也受到关注。
鹏华基⾦⾸席资产配置官郑科认为，AI相关资产对市场资⾦产⽣的虹吸效应，也是本轮⾦价回撤

的影响因素之⼀。
AI产业的融资需求已从股票市场延伸⾄信贷市场。汇丰资产管理11⽉发布的研究显⽰，截⾄今
年9⽉，具有投资级评级的⼤型云计算服务商已发⾏约2500亿美元债券，预计2027年仍将有相
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
51 收藏 分享到:
写评论
近规模的发⾏。
⽩雪⽯指出，尽管过去⼀年主要央⾏的货币政策有所收紧，但AI资本开⽀推动了企业融资及银⾏
信贷扩张。按照其货币信⽤扩张与⻩⾦定价的分析框架，⻩⾦的表现本不应该如此疲弱。
不过，与能够⻓期存续、交易和传承的房地产等资产不同，AI相关设备不仅⾯临较快的折旧，还
可能因技术迭代提前失去经济价值。
在⽩雪⽯看来，⻩⾦的疲弱表现也可能提⽰投资者重新审视AI投资所形成的财富价值。
基于⻩⾦作为财富价值标尺的逻辑，⽩雪⽯尝试通过全球主要央⾏的货币发⾏规模与地表⻩⾦
存量的关系进⾏估值，并认为当前⻩⾦即期价格存在相对偏低估。
若全球私⼈部⻔的⻩⾦配置⽐例达到⽬前全球央⾏约19%的配置⽔平，⽩雪⽯认为，⻩⾦仍存
在较⼤的上⾏空间。不过，这更多代表⼀种估值上限情景，现实中并不可能⼀步到位。

⻩⾦具备⻓期配置价值，并不意味着其在任何价格下都具有同样的投资吸引⼒。
郑科认为，在全球货币体系演变的过渡阶段，⻩⾦可以成为衡量其他资产价值的重要参照。但
对于⼈⺠币投资者⽽⾔，当⾦价达到价值锚点，其作⽤可能更多体现为波段操作及对冲组合波
动。
在中国，⻩⾦需求正在进⼀步向投资端倾斜。
世界⻩⾦协会数据显⽰，今年前三季度，中国⾦条⾦币投资需求达393吨，创历年同期新⾼，⽽
同期⾦饰消费需求则同⽐下降27%⾄202吨。
贾舒畅认为，政府债务问题、全球货币多极化及地缘政治碎⽚化等⻓期因素并未消失。投资者
可以将⻩⾦作为战略性底仓，并根据经济周期及资产波动情况动态调整配置⽐例。

## Document 522: 中央銀行と闘うな 利上げ局面で株式は弱い

Source PDF: all report pdf/中央銀行と闘うな 利上げ局面で株式は弱い.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026年 11月 17日(火)

1/4 
中央銀行と闘うな 利上げ局面で株式は弱い    
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・最近のＡＩ・半導体株を中心とする世界的な株価調整は、米中間選挙における民主党の躍進
に伴うＡＩ投資の増勢鈍化懸念、エヌビディアＣＥＯのスキャンダルおよび同氏の辞任に絡
んだ「商慣行」の見直し、そして対中半導体規制強化（半導体へのリモートアクセスを禁止
する動き）のほか、ごく直近ではアンソロピック社の大型ＩＰＯに伴う換金売りなどが背景
にある。何れもＡＩ・半導体関連株に対して逆風の材料である。

・そうしたなか、世界的にＡＩ・半導体銘柄のＰＥＲは顕著に低下している。ＰＥＲ低下の数
値的な背景は、予想ＥＰＳが切り上がるなかで株価が下落しているからである。S&P500にお
ける「情報技術セクター」や、世界の代表的な半導体株で構成されるＳＯＸ指数などのＰＥ
Ｒが注目されている。こうしたＰＥＲ低下を割安感の台頭（割高感の解消）と読み好感すべ
きか、それともＡＩ投資の減速を織り込んだ業績悪化懸念の広がりと読むべきか、これが目
下の最大の関心事である。

・日経平均株価は先行き 12ヶ月 72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き 12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を 12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに 2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利（誘導目標上限）を、12月に4.25％へ引き上げるだろう

### Page 2

2/4

・ＰＥＲ低下はＡＩの将来に対する文脈で語られがちだが、別の角度から説明することもでき
る。あまりにも単純過ぎると𠮟られてしまいそうだが、それはＦｅｄを中心とする主要中銀
の利上げである。奇しくも日米欧中銀は９月に同時利上げを実施し、12月にも再度同時利上
げとなる公算が現時点では大きい。日銀も10月展望レポートで「世界的にインフレ圧力が高
まるなか、夏場以降、米欧を含む多くの中央銀行は利上げ方向に政策を転換しており、こう
したグローバルな金融環境の変化が、国際金融市場等を通じてわが国の経済・物価に及ぼす
影響には留意が必要である」と記述していた。2024～2025年は日銀が利上げを続けていたの
をよそに、欧米中銀が利下げ方向に動いていたため、世界全体でみれば金融環境は緩和方向
にあった。それに対して2026年後半以降、主要中銀は同時に金融引き締め方向に動いてお
り、世界全体として株式市場にストレスがかかりやすい状況にある。金利上昇による資金調
達コストの増加がＡＩ投資そのものを減速させ得る経路もあれば、安全資産である国債利回
りの上昇が株式の相対的な魅力を低下させ株価を下押しするという経路もある。

・ここで米10年実質金利とS&P500やNASDAQの予想ＰＥＲの推移を確認すると、この間のＰＥＲ
低下（≒株価下落）は実質金利の上昇に沿っていることがわかる。実質金利は５月に２％を
超えた後、Ｆｅｄの利上げ観測の高まりに伴って現在は2.9％近傍まで水準を切り上げてお
り、これが株式のバリュエーション（ＰＥＲ）を下押ししている可能性が高い。Ｆｅｄの金
融引き締めがＰＥＲ低下を招いた例としては2018年と2022年があり、今回も利上げが株価へ
打撃を与えるという単純な結果になりつつある。「中央銀行と闘うな」という格言が思い出
される。

S&P500 予想ＰＥＲ（先行き12ヶ月）
